- REL1D-A implemented at `d1d4708d7e491131d81ced19de4495bef0c0ddc5`. A user can attach `.png`, `.jpg`, `.jpeg`, or `.webp` through the existing multipart `/resources/register` path with `ingest_content=false`. The file is stored under the upload root, current user, and object id, with a SHA-256 content hash. Magic bytes are checked before accept: PNG signature, JPEG SOI, or RIFF/WEBP. SVG, GIF, BMP, HEIC, and renamed arbitrary bytes are rejected. Text, Markdown, CSV, and Parquet registration is unchanged. `POST /people/role-import/extract` accepts only `source_object_id`. An image source must be the current user's active upload, stay inside that object directory, match its suffix family, and recompute to the stored hash. Client path metadata is never opened. A text source uses one stored `full` representation, otherwise ordered `chunk` rows, otherwise the object body. Nothing is fetched from a URL, provider, or local path. Text is clipped at 24000 characters and then `source_truncated` is set. The image revision is the SHA-256 of the exact bytes. The text revision is the SHA-256 of the exact UTF-8 sent onward. The production provider is one Responses call with `store=false`, no tools, and structured JSON, using the current user's OpenAI credential and effective assistant model plus the existing daily budget guard. Tests use a scripted provider only. The fixed instruction treats the source as untrusted and forbids following embedded commands, inventing people, or deciding matches, RoleTerms, promotion, or importance. The proposal has `source_object_id`, `source_revision`, `source_kind` `image|text`, `source_truncated`, at most 32 items, and `items_truncated`. Each item is an ungrounded `person_name`, `role`, optional `context`, `evidence_text`, and optional `source_locator`. Exact displayed duplicates collapse. Different visible casing stays separate. The response has no Person, RoleTerm, or assignment id. Extraction does not change Person, role, identity, edge, task, label, notification, or ActionPlan rows. Audit metadata records workload `role_import_extraction`, source kind, lengths, revision, item count, truncation, and token counts, and does not record image bytes, source text, names, roles, or evidence. The Assistant shows `Извлечь роли` only after an object context is set. Attach does not extract. The preview is headed `Черновик извлечения ролей`, says `Ничего не сохранено`, and has no save or approve control. Changing context drops a stale preview, including a late response for the previous source. Ordinary send still posts text plus the object id. Tests: `test_rel1d_role_import_source.py` and `test_rel1d_role_import_extraction.py` 33 passed, 0 failed. Flutter `role_import_preview_test.dart` 4 passed; `local_intake_actions_test.dart` and `local_file_intake_privacy_test.dart` 12 passed; 0 failed. Focused analyze of the changed Dart found no new issues. Ruff passed. `py_compile` passed. `git diff --check` clean. The combined resource, format-parity, REL1A, REL1C-A, REL1C-B, and AI-audit run was 115 passed and 4 failed. Those 4 also fail when `resource_registration_service.py` is restored to the pre-REL1D-A revision: `test_register_same_revision_already_ingested_skips_content`, `test_register_long_text_chunks_embedded_by_worker_and_ranked_in_context`, `test_metadata_only_upload_persisted_for_later_ingest`, and `test_mixed_workload_summary_metrics`. Schema head remains `0054`. No deploy, migration, client install, real model call, or provider call. Production and the installed client remain `6f802d6959aca40758376a83d5bdfcbbd77fc537`, Alembic `0054 / 0054`. REL1D-B and REL1D-C were not started.

- REL1D-B implemented at `c0599932a9c917e0efde51d237d8cb3df79c5a88`. `POST /people/role-import/ground` accepts only `source_object_id`, `source_revision`, and at most 32 extraction rows. Person grounding reuses `PersonAssistantService.resolve` once per distinct name: `resolved`, `ambiguous` sorted by title then Person id, or, only when the resolver returns none, up to 3 exact display-name promotion candidates from one `eligible_direct_contacts` scan. That scan keeps the existing repeated-direct-contact, owned-identity, suppression, and Telegram gates. The candidate key is a SHA-256 of the identity tuple and does not expose the canonical value. A screenshot or document name alone is not a promotion candidate. Role grounding batch-loads exact REL1A terms: an exact key is `reuse_existing`; anything else is `propose_new` with at most 5 lexical suggestions. `grounding_revision` is a SHA-256 of the canonical grounded JSON and changes when the source, Person grounding, RoleTerm grounding, or row text changes. The endpoint does not write product rows or AI traces and does not call a model. The client shows `Сопоставить` only after an explicit extraction with rows. Grounding is not automatic. The preview still says `Ничего не сохранено` and has no save, approve, apply, or create control. A stale source asks to extract again. Context change, re-extraction, and a late response drop old grounding. - REL1D-C1 implemented at `3d3bead03271bad5dfc5713eaf78a4d1062cb11e`. `POST /people/role-import/action-plan` reruns REL1D-B grounding for an explicit selection and stores one pending ActionPlan. The only action is `apply_role_import_batch`: `INTERNAL_WRITE`, not Assistant-exposed, not MCP-exposed, and refused unless the write mode is approved. A resolved row freezes that Person id. An ambiguous row freezes only a grounded candidate. A promotion row freezes the grounded candidate key. An unresolved row cannot be selected. The server freezes `reuse_existing` or `create_if_missing`; suggestions never become the selected term. Frozen rows keep the extracted name, target display, role, context, and vocabulary mode, and omit evidence, source locators, raw identities, normalized keys, and salience. Approval locks the user row, the source Object, and the Representation rows the text source actually uses, reloads the source, and fails the whole plan on `role_import_source_changed` or `role_import_grounding_changed`. Promotion reuses one Person for every selected role of the same candidate. Role execution reuses an exact term that appears after staging and leaves semantic near-duplicates distinct. Duplicate selected rows share one assignment. The same role with a different context stays distinct. The active cap remains 16. `assign_outcome` accepts an optional `source_object_id` and writes it only on a newly created row. New import assignments use `origin=user`, `provenance_kind=role_import_confirmed`, and `role-import:<operation_id>`. Manual and REL1C provenance stay unchanged on a no-op. The existing ActionPlan savepoint is the atomic boundary: a later-row failure rolls back earlier promotions, identities, evidence, RoleTerms, and assignments from that plan. Reject and expiry write nothing. The approval card shows the source title, selected and total counts, truncation flags, and at most 32 rows of target display, target mode, role, context, and vocabulary mode. Execution effects report changed or no-op from `people_created` and `assignments_changed` only. Tests: `test_rel1d_role_import_batch.py` 31 passed, 0 failed. Reject shows `Отклонено` and keeps `Ничего не сохранено`. Expiry shows `Подтверждение истекло`. Failure shows `Ошибка применения` and does not claim a partial save. - REL1D-HG1.4 implemented at `92f8ddf5b5898dd00ba4f5a244d067a1a30c4f50`. Files: `backend/app/domain/role_import_participants.py`, `backend/app/services/person_promotion_service.py`, `backend/app/services/person_role_import_grounding_service.py`, `backend/app/services/person_role_import_batch_service.py`, `backend/tests/test_rel1d_role_import_participants.py`, `backend/tests/test_rel1d_role_import_grounding.py`, `backend/tests/test_rel1d_role_import_batch.py`, `client/lib/assistant/role_import_preview.dart`, `client/test/assistant/role_import_preview_test.dart`. A not-yet-Person extracted name becomes a role-import promotion candidate only when one stored communication already carries that exact normalized display name plus an attachable identity: inbound or outbound email sender/from, to/recipients, or cc, and reply-to only with its own display name; Mattermost author user id or username with author display name, including channel authors and excluding the user's own author; Teams user sender with tenant, sender id, and display name, including group chats and excluding non-user senders; Telegram MTProto inbound sender user id plus sender display name in private or group when the existing Assistant AI gate allows the object, and an outbound private peer id plus stored peer title, never a group or channel title. Bare addresses, body or subject text, and a display name without an identity do not qualify. One such hit is enough. The scan uses `PERSON_LOOKBACK_DAYS` and `MAX_PERSON_SCAN_ROWS`; a hit inside a truncated scan stays, and a name with zero hits in that scan is omitted. Existing-Person HG1.2 gating is unchanged, and a filtered graph row does not fall through to a new Person. An identity already bound to an active Person is not offered as a new-Person candidate; if name resolution already returns that Person, the existing-Person path is used. `overview()`, `eligible_direct_contacts()`, generic `approve()`, and `MIN_DIRECT_HITS` stay at the historical threshold of 2. Approval re-reads `eligible_role_import_participants()` and calls `approve_role_import_participant()` for the frozen candidate key and exact display name. Missing or changed evidence fails with `role_import_grounding_changed` and writes no Person or role. A bound identity fails closed and does not create a duplicate. Public ActionPlan arguments stay empty. The client shows the safe display name, provider, and `N в переписке`, and still requires an explicit candidate choice. Tests: `test_rel1d_role_import_grounding.py` 27 passed, `test_rel1d_role_import_batch.py` 38 passed, `test_rel1d_role_import_participants.py` 17 passed, `test_person_promotion.py` 25 passed, together 107 passed, 0 failed, plus the pre-existing `HTTP_422_UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY` warning in `test_http_source_conflict_detail`. Flutter `role_import_preview_test.dart`, `role_import_plan_test.dart`, and `role_import_scroll_test.dart` together 18 passed, 0 failed. Ruff check of the touched Python files passed. `flutter analyze` of `role_import_preview.dart` and `role_import_preview_test.dart`: no issues. `git diff --check` clean. No deploy, client install, migration, model call, provider call, or product-data action. Production backend, ref, and the installed client remain `1879aabc97bfc5ed13fbc2ffa4d0c9c7ccbb3320`. Alembic remains `0054 / 0054`.

- REL1D-HG1.4 ARCHITECT REVIEW: NOT YET ACCEPTED. Implementation `92f8ddf5b5898dd00ba4f5a244d067a1a30c4f50` correctly introduces role-import-specific participant-backed promotion and separate approval revalidation while leaving generic `MIN_DIRECT_HITS=2` promotion unchanged, but review found an incomplete own-identity exclusion. Telegram self id is filtered; Mattermost filters remote_user_id but not robustly the account username path; Google/Yandex mail account addresses and Teams account `(tenant_id, microsoft_user_id)` are not excluded from the role-import participant scan. This can offer the user's own stored identity as a new Person when an organization-chart display name matches. HG1.4 requires narrow HG1.4.1 self-identity corrective before source acceptance. No rollout authorized.
- REL1D-HG1.4.1 implemented at `9a9e342ba3ce78cd8b4b0421724d34769569453d`. Files: `backend/app/domain/role_import_participants.py`, `backend/app/services/person_promotion_service.py`, `backend/tests/test_rel1d_role_import_participants.py`. The role-import participant scan drops an identity only when its normalized provider, type, realm, and value match a current-user account: every `GoogleAccount.email`, every `YandexMailAccount.email`, `TeamsAccount` `tenant_id` plus `microsoft_user_id`, each `MattermostAccount` `remote_user_id` and `username` on that server realm, and each Telegram MTProto account user id on that account id. The same Teams user id in another tenant and the same Mattermost id or username on another server stay eligible. A different exact identity with the user's display name stays eligible. Display name is not self-proof. `approve_role_import_participant()` uses the same scan, so an account row added after prepare fails the plan with `role_import_grounding_changed` and writes no Person or role assignment. `overview()`, `eligible_direct_contacts()`, generic `approve()`, and `MIN_DIRECT_HITS` are unchanged, and a non-self one-hit participant still qualifies. Tests: `test_rel1d_role_import_participants.py` 25 passed, `test_rel1d_role_import_grounding.py` 27 passed, `test_rel1d_role_import_batch.py` 38 passed, `test_person_promotion.py` 25 passed, together 115 passed, 0 failed, plus the pre-existing `HTTP_422_UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY` warning in `test_http_source_conflict_detail`. Ruff check of the touched Python files passed. `git diff --check` clean. No client change, deploy, install, migration, model call, provider call, or product-data action. Production backend, ref, and the installed client remain `1879aabc97bfc5ed13fbc2ffa4d0c9c7ccbb3320`. Alembic remains `0054 / 0054`.

HG1.4 implementation `92f8ddf5b5898dd00ba4f5a244d067a1a30c4f50` adds role-import-specific one-hit identity-bearing stored communication participant discovery and approval revalidation while leaving generic People promotion (`MIN_DIRECT_HITS=2`, `overview()`, `eligible_direct_contacts()`, generic `approve()`) unchanged. HG1.4.1 implementation `9a9e342ba3ce78cd8b4b0421724d34769569453d` completes exact own-identity exclusion across Google/Gmail email, Yandex Mail email, Teams tenant+user id, Mattermost server-scoped remote id/username, and Telegram MTProto account-scoped user id; display-name equality alone never marks a participant as self, and same raw id/name in another realm remains eligible. Approve-time revalidation uses the same self-aware participant scan, so an identity that becomes self-owned after prepare fails with `role_import_grounding_changed` and writes no Person/role. - REL1D-HG-D1 ARCHITECT REVIEW: diagnosis accepted. For `Шабаршина Ирина Сергеевна`, normal Person resolution is `none`; the HG1.4 participant scan has zero exact-display identity-bearing candidates; within the bounded 90-day scan there are 12 Gmail communication rows where the exact name appears only in searchable title/body/subject text, with no named sender/reply-to identity and no Mattermost/Teams/Telegram participant evidence. Root cause is therefore `name_only_in_searchable_text`, not self/owned/suppression filtering and not a proven recipient-normalization loss for this person. Product boundary refined: role import may use a second, weaker communication-evidence tier for not-yet-Person names. One exact identity-bearing participant hit remains sufficient for identity-backed promotion. Separately, at least two distinct stored communication objects containing the exact normalized multi-token person name may support an explicit **name-only Person candidate** when normal Person resolution is none and no stronger identity-backed candidate is available. Approval creates only the Person + selected role assignment; it MUST NOT invent or attach an email/user-id identity. The candidate must be clearly labelled as mention-backed / contact identity unknown, remain explicit-choice and ActionPlan-gated, and be revalidated at approve time. Source-only org-chart names, a single text mention, fuzzy/variant/semantic name matching, and arbitrary inferred endpoint binding remain forbidden. This fallback is role-import-specific and must not weaken generic Person promotion or global identity semantics. Existing resolved Person rows may use the same exact-name mention evidence only as a role-import communication gate fallback; ambiguous rows may use it only for candidates whose displayed title exactly matches the extracted name, preserving explicit ambiguity. Strong identity-backed evidence supersedes mention-backed fallback. Human REL1D acceptance remains paused pending implementation and rollout of this corrective.
- REL1D-HG1.5 implemented at `8f3c748a805956eeed137d67ee1c75b81e49fb25`. Files: `backend/app/domain/role_import_mentions.py`, `backend/app/services/person_role_import_mention_service.py`, `backend/app/services/person_role_import_grounding_service.py`, `backend/app/services/person_role_import_batch_service.py`, `backend/app/services/person_role_import_batch_models.py`, `backend/app/assistant/approval_presentation.py`, `backend/tests/test_rel1d_role_import_mentions.py`, `client/lib/api/role_import_models.dart`, `client/lib/assistant/role_import_preview.dart`, `client/test/assistant/role_import_preview_test.dart`. A not-yet-Person extracted name stays on the HG1.4 identity-participant path when that exact display has an attachable identity. Otherwise, `MIN_ROLE_IMPORT_NAME_MENTION_OBJECTS = 2` distinct active email or chat objects in the same 90-day `MAX_PERSON_SCAN_ROWS` scan, including the Telegram Assistant gate, can expose one mention candidate. The match is the exact collapsed casefolded multi-token name, with flexible internal whitespace and Unicode token boundaries, in title, body, or email subject only. One object, a single token, a partial name, or a longer-token substring does not qualify. Zero or one hit inside a truncated scan fails closed. The candidate key is a SHA-256 of the prefix `role-import-name-mention` plus the normalized display name, with no object id or identity. Approval creates one Person through `create_person` and the selected role, and attaches no PersonIdentity and no PersonIdentityEvidence. The same candidate in one batch reuses that Person. A later-row failure rolls the name-only Person back. If resolution is no longer `none`, an identity-backed candidate appears, or the mention count drops below 2, approval fails with `role_import_grounding_changed` and writes nothing. A resolved Person with zero attributable communications stays actionable only when the title exactly matches and mention evidence meets the threshold. Ambiguous candidates with zero attributable communications stay ambiguous and only when their titles exactly match; a different-title variant is omitted. The client says `Упомянут в переписке: N сообщений`, `Контактная личность не установлена`, and `После подтверждения будет создан новый Person без привязки контакта`. The plan mode is `name_mentions`. Public ActionPlan arguments stay empty. `MIN_DIRECT_HITS`, `overview()`, `eligible_direct_contacts()`, generic `approve()`, and the HG1.4 one-hit participant rule are unchanged. - REL1D-HG1.5 ARCHITECT REVIEW: implementation `8f3c748a805956eeed137d67ee1c75b81e49fb25` is NOT YET source-accepted due to one narrow bounded-scan contract violation. Grounding correctly calls `PersonRoleImportMentionEvidenceService.evidence_for(...)` once for all extracted names and has a test proving that. However, ActionPlan execution creates one mention service and then `_mention_person(...)` calls `evidence_for([row.extracted_person_name])` separately for each distinct mention-backed selected row. Therefore approval can rescan the same bounded communication universe once per selected name, contrary to the explicit HG1.5 requirement that the requested-name set be evaluated in one bounded DB scan. This does not invalidate the observed 137/0 backend result or the mention semantics; it requires a narrow HG1.5.1 execution-revalidation batching corrective. Required shape: after the existing user serialization lock and source validation, collect all selected `evidence_kind=name_mentions` names, call `evidence_for(unique_names)` exactly once, then revalidate each mention-backed row against that map. Strong identity participant revalidation, normal Person resolution, candidate key/display checks, zero-identity creation, atomic rollback, generic promotion, client behavior, schema, and production state must remain unchanged. No rollout authorized until this corrective is reviewed.

- ARCHITECT DECISION after human REL1D evidence: after active D2 completes, run a cross-connector **per-user, per-account participant-identity coverage audit** before authorizing further connector or grounding fixes. Audit unit is `Secretary user × connector account/connection × communication provider/kind`, not provider-wide aggregates. Communication-producing connectors in current scope: Google/Gmail, Yandex Mail, Mattermost, Microsoft Teams, Telegram MTProto. For every connected account/realm, measure: stored communication Object count and date coverage; sync-start/backfill coverage; stable remote participant identity availability; human display-name availability and source fields; self-identity availability; how many stored rows produce a normalized participant identity through current `participant_identities(...)`; how many rows have stable id but only technical username/no human name; how many have human name but no attachable id; how many are text-only mentions; how many strong identities are lost only because of the shared `MAX_PERSON_SCAN_ROWS=400` cap; and whether Search-visible evidence comes from native provider Objects versus email notifications/forwarded summaries. Preserve account/realm separation: multiple Google/Yandex/Mattermost connections must be audited independently and raw ids/addresses/tokens must remain out of ledger/report. Do not auto-merge a human across providers/accounts during the audit. D2 remains active and unchanged; no new implementation is authorized until D2 + the broader audit establish which failures are ingestion/normalization, history coverage, scan starvation, or expected absence of identity.

- REL1D-HG-D2 ARCHITECT REVIEW: diagnosis accepted. Full 90-day communication universe is 4481 Objects; the shared role-import window of the newest `MAX_PERSON_SCAN_ROWS=400` is truncated with oldest included timestamp `2026-09-30T13:50:46.933000+00:00`, so the target Teams messages from 2026-09-18/19 are outside the scan. No provider, model, or sync call. Session rolled back. Account and product counts unchanged. No raw ids, addresses, usernames, server URLs, or tokens recorded.

- REL1D-HG-D3 ARCHITECT REVIEW: audit accepted. D3 establishes that the remaining REL1D human-gate failures are upstream identity-pipeline defects, not a reason to further widen weak name matching. Accepted per-connection findings: Gmail/Yandex lose recipient/cc human display and have substantial legacy account-provenance gaps; Mattermost has stable author id/username on 2526 rows but zero human author display; Teams has 28 identity-ready rows missing `sender_kind` plus most parsed identities outside the shared newest-400 window; MTProto has large inbound sender-id coverage with missing human sender display plus most emitted identities outside the window; Telegram Business rows are unsupported by the current participant parser. The global newest-400 cross-provider window is independently defective because it is dominated by one high-volume provider and excludes strong identity-ready rows from every provider with usable identities. Corrective sequence is now fixed: (A) provider-neutral role-import scan starvation corrective, keeping generic Person/Assistant scan semantics unchanged; (B) connector normalization/parser-contract source correctives, split into bounded provider-focused tasks; (C) controlled provider refresh/backfill of already stored legacy rows only after source contracts are accepted and deployed; then resume human REL1D acceptance. Mention-backed HG1.5 remains a genuine weak-evidence fallback and must not be broadened further to compensate for connector defects.
